Will the metal bend at high speeds?</p><div class="picnic--c-PJLV picnic--c-PJLV-iiQbdEO-css"></div></div><div data-orientation="horizontal" role="separator" class="picnic--c-iSjnJf picnic--c-iSjnJf-ikMWpmy-css"></div><p class="picnic--c-cyRcZm picnic--c-cyRcZm-YfiYb-variant-caption">The Carver Bimini tops can be safely used and deployed at speeds of up to 35 MPH. If you would like to go faster with your top-up, we recommend thru-bolting.
</p></div><div tabindex="0" aria-label="What is the difference between precision and custom fit seat covers?" class="picnic--c-kegcFP picnic--c-fDpkJ picnic--c-kegcFP-hRoyeA-interactive-true picnic--c-fDpkJ-ghGoeW-templateType-company"><div class="picnic--c-PJLV picnic--c-PJLV-ikMQKoj-css"><p class="picnic--c-cyRcZm picnic--c-cyRcZm-YfiYb-variant-caption">What is the difference between precision and custom fit seat covers?</p><div class="picnic--c-PJLV picnic--c-PJLV-iiQbdEO-css"></div></div><div data-orientation="horizontal" role="separator" class="picnic--c-iSjnJf picnic--c-iSjnJf-ikMWpmy-css"></div><p class="picnic--c-cyRcZm picnic--c-cyRcZm-YfiYb-variant-caption">We offer various types of seat covers, most of which are tailored specifically for each vehicle. PrecisionFit seat covers are snug-fitting and foam-backed, except for the Spacer Mesh material. These cannot be machine-washed due to the foam.

Marathon Excel seat covers are also snug fitting, made from our toughest material, Cordura, and are machine washable.

SeatSaver seat covers provide a great fit, but they are designed to be easily installed and removed for convenient machine washing. 

Bucket seat covers are typically a one-piece design with pillows that secure in between the seats for added security. Other designs are two-piece bucket seats, with a separate bottom and top that allow for a snug fit.

Anything labeled as Ready-Fit is a semi-custom design, so the fit may not be as precise as custom covers. These designs accommodate a wider range of patterns.</p></div><div tabindex="0" aria-label="Sales Collection" class="picnic--c-kegcFP picnic--c-fDpkJ picnic--c-kegcFP-hRoyeA-interactive-true picnic--c-fDpkJ-ghGoeW-templateType-company"><div class="picnic--c-PJLV picnic--c-PJLV-ikMQKoj-css"><p class="picnic--c-cyRcZm picnic--c-cyRcZm-YfiYb-variant-caption">Sales Collection</p><div class="picnic--c-PJLV picnic--c-PJLV-iiQbdEO-css"><div class="picnic--c-PJLV picnic--c-PJLV-ibIyLZu-css"><p class="picnic--c-cyRcZm picnic--c-cyRcZm-XTsru-variant-micro">spg</p></div></div></div><div data-orientation="horizontal" role="separator" class="picnic--c-iSjnJf picnic--c-iSjnJf-ikMWpmy-css"></div><p class="picnic--c-cyRcZm picnic--c-cyRcZm-YfiYb-variant-caption">You can take a look at our sale collection here: https://www.covercraft.com/c/special-offers</p></div><div tabindex="0" aria-label="Do you have boat covers?" class="picnic--c-kegcFP picnic--c-fDpkJ picnic--c-kegcFP-hRoyeA-interactive-true picnic--c-fDpkJ-ghGoeW-templateType-company"><div class="picnic--c-PJLV picnic--c-PJLV-ikMQKoj-css"><p class="picnic--c-cyRcZm picnic--c-cyRcZm-YfiYb-variant-caption">Do you have boat covers?</p><div class="picnic--c-PJLV picnic--c-PJLV-iiQbdEO-css"></div></div><div data-orientation="horizontal" role="separator" class="picnic--c-iSjnJf picnic--c-iSjnJf-ikMWpmy-css"></div><p class="picnic--c-cyRcZm picnic--c-cyRcZm-YfiYb-variant-caption">We do have boat covers! The difference between the UVS100 Original and the UVS100 Premier Series Custom Sunscreens is mainly in the appearance and included accessories, not performance.

Both are custom-fit to your vehicle's windshield and built with the same core materials: a reflective outer layer, a foam core for insulation, and a soft felt backing. Both provide excellent heat and UV protection and help keep your interior cooler.

The Premier Series, however, adds a more upscale look. It features a black backing and black binding around the edges, while the Original has a lighter gray color. The Premier also comes in premium finishes like Chrome Camouflage, Galaxy Silver, and white, which give it a more stylish appearance.

In short, both perform the same in terms of sun and heat protection, but the Premier Series offers a more refined design and added accessories. If aesthetics and presentation matter to you, the Premier is the better option. If you're focused strictly on function, the Original will serve you just as well. Hope this helps!</p></div><div tabindex="0" aria-label="What is my Sub-Model / Can I find my Sub-Model using VIN?" class="picnic--c-kegcFP picnic--c-fDpkJ picnic--c-kegcFP-hRoyeA-interactive-true picnic--c-fDpkJ-ghGoeW-templateType-company"><div class="picnic--c-PJLV picnic--c-PJLV-ikMQKoj-css"><p class="picnic--c-cyRcZm picnic--c-cyRcZm-YfiYb-variant-caption">What is my Sub-Model / Can I find my Sub-Model using VIN?</p><div class="picnic--c-PJLV picnic--c-PJLV-iiQbdEO-css"></div></div><div data-orientation="horizontal" role="separator" class="picnic--c-iSjnJf picnic--c-iSjnJf-ikMWpmy-css"></div><p class="picnic--c-cyRcZm picnic--c-cyRcZm-YfiYb-variant-caption"> Yes! This will be your sub-model.</p></div><div tabindex="0" aria-label="Do you sell waterproof car covers?" class="picnic--c-kegcFP picnic--c-fDpkJ picnic--c-kegcFP-hRoyeA-interactive-true picnic--c-fDpkJ-ghGoeW-templateType-company"><div class="picnic--c-PJLV picnic--c-PJLV-ikMQKoj-css"><p class="picnic--c-cyRcZm picnic--c-cyRcZm-YfiYb-variant-caption">Do you sell waterproof car covers?</p><div class="picnic--c-PJLV picnic--c-PJLV-iiQbdEO-css"></div></div><div data-orientation="horizontal" role="separator" class="picnic--c-iSjnJf picnic--c-iSjnJf-ikMWpmy-css"></div><p class="picnic--c-cyRcZm picnic--c-cyRcZm-YfiYb-variant-caption">We don't sell waterproof covers, and that's by design. Since 1965, we've been trusted by car owners to protect their vehicles with covers that are carefully engineered for long-term care. One of the biggest lessons we've learned in six decades of craftsmanship is that waterproof materials can do more harm than good. A true waterproof fabric doesn't breathe. That means any moisture, humidity, or gases trapped underneath have nowhere to escape. The result? Rust, corrosion, and even mold-the very problems you're trying to avoid. That's why especially our outdoor car covers are made with water-resistant, breathable fabrics designed to: 

• Shed most rainfall and block moisture from seeping in 
• Allow trapped heat and humidity to escape, protecting finishes and metalwork 
• Prevent rust and corrosion, keeping your vehicle safe for the long run 

By balancing water resistance with breathability, we ensure your cover protects without trapping damage underneath. So, while you won't find a "waterproof" car cover here, you'll get something better: a cover built with over 60 years of expertise-engineered to safeguard your vehicle the right way. View each material water repellency rating for which is best for your needs.</p></div><div tabindex="0" aria-label="Do Your Seat Covers Come with a Console Cover?" class="picnic--c-kegcFP picnic--c-fDpkJ picnic--c-kegcFP-hRoyeA-interactive-true picnic--c-fDpkJ-ghGoeW-templateType-company"><div class="picnic--c-PJLV picnic--c-PJLV-ikMQKoj-css"><p class="picnic--c-cyRcZm picnic--c-cyRcZm-YfiYb-variant-caption">Do Your Seat Covers Come with a Console Cover?</p><div class="picnic--c-PJLV picnic--c-PJLV-iiQbdEO-css"></div></div><div data-orientation="horizontal" role="separator" class="picnic--c-iSjnJf picnic--c-iSjnJf-ikMWpmy-css"></div><p class="picnic--c-cyRcZm picnic--c-cyRcZm-YfiYb-variant-caption">The answer depends on the seat cover line and the specific pattern for your vehicle.
